USER ../elm_specs_md/pilot_kit/chunks/problem_statement.md

USER # Employee Leave Management (ELM)
USER 
USER Synchronized Problem Statement and Controlled Context
USER 
USER Status: FINAL SYNCHRONIZED BASELINE | Version 1.4
USER 
USER > Revision basis
USER This final synchronized baseline incorporates the 18 domain adjudications made after the Phase-2 materials, the coherent-CFR / atomic-AFR requirements arrangement, and the finalized UC/WF formalism. The historical SRS-EL-001…107 set remains the immutable as-administered student baseline and is preserved through the companion crosswalk.
USER 
USER ## 1. Context
USER 
USER Fusion is the institute ERP used to provide administrative, academic, student, and related services. Users may hold one or more roles, and access to ELM capabilities is controlled through role-based permissions.
USER 
USER ELM supports leave-related activities for institute employees. Employees are Faculty or Staff, each with a designation and an organizational unit such as a Department, Section, or Office. Each organizational unit has a Unit Head. Depending on leave type and hierarchy, the Unit Head either gives the final decision or reviews and records Recommended / Not Recommended before the request moves through the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER ## 2. Leave categories and Station Leave
USER 
USER ELM supports CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, and VL. Station Leave permission/details are captured within the same leave application when the employee will be outside Jabalpur; Station Leave is not a separate leave-request process.
USER 
USER ## 3. Entitlement, applicability, and carry-forward
USER 
USER CL: 8 days/year for each employee; first-half and second-half CL are supported; an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day; unused CL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER RH: 2 days/year for each employee; an RH request must correspond to an applicable Restricted Holiday in the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar; unused RH does not carry forward.
USER 
USER SCL: up to 15 days/year for each employee for a special purpose considered admissible by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility rule is required in this baseline; unused SCL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER EL: non-faculty employees receive 30 days/year directly; unused EL carries forward.
USER 
USER VL: faculty receive 60 VL associated with vacation periods supplied through the institute Academic Calendar. VL may be availed only during an applicable vacation period. At year end, all unused VL converts to EL at 2 VL : 1 EL, including fractional conversion. VL itself does not carry forward; converted EL is credited to the faculty member’s EL balance, may carry forward, and may later be availed.
USER 
USER COL: 20 days/year in this baseline for each eligible employee; COL is medical/balance-controlled leave, supporting medical evidence may be required where applicable, and unused COL carries forward.
USER 
USER ## 4. Leave-day counting
USER 
USER For EL, COL, and VL, leave is counted continuously across the approved leave interval. Intervening weekends and closed holidays within the interval count as leave days. This continuing-count rule does not apply to CL, SCL, or RH.
USER 
USER ## 5. Applying for leave and validation
USER 
USER The employee submits one leave application containing leave category, requested period, purpose/reason, and other required information. Station Leave details are included in the same application when applicable.
USER 
USER Before a new request is allowed to proceed, ELM validates the employee’s leave-category eligibility, available entitlement/balance, applicable leave-counting and calendar conditions, and impermissible overlap with the employee’s active pending or approved leave. First-half and second-half CL on the same date are not treated as overlapping when they occupy different halves.
USER 
USER Leave-policy parameters do not use a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle in this baseline. Validation uses the current configured leave-policy parameters. Date and RH validation use the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar.
USER 
USER ## 6. Substitute / responsibility coverage
USER 
USER When responsibility coverage is required, the applicant nominates one substitute. The substitute must be another employee, must not be the applicant, and must not have overlapping pending or approved leave during the responsibility period.
USER 
USER The nominated substitute may accept or decline the assignment. Required substitute consent must be accepted before approval routing continues. If the substitute declines, the applicant may nominate another eligible substitute or withdraw the pending request.
USER 
USER ## 7. Review, sanction, and decision communication
USER 
USER For CL and RH, including associated Station Leave, the request is routed to the applicant’s Unit Head and the Unit Head is the final authority.
USER 
USER For SCL, EL, COL, and VL, the Unit Head reviews the request and records Recommended or Not Recommended. Not Recommended is not a final rejection for these categories. The request then proceeds through the configured hierarchy to the appropriate final Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER The processing/sanctioning path is resolved from the applicant, organizational unit, designation/role, leave category, and current hierarchy configuration. Named roles may include Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director. An Establishment processing step occurs where configured. The Director’s own leave follows the Director-specific self-sanction rule.
USER 
USER On a final decision, the decision is recorded and the employee is notified. On final approval, the employee’s leave record/account is also updated. Intermediate decisions and state changes need not generate general notifications; the employee can inspect them through leave status/history.
USER 
USER ## 8. Pending request, withdrawal, and approved-leave cancellation
USER 
USER A submitted pending leave request is not modified in place. If a change is required before final approval, the employee may withdraw the pending request and submit a new application if needed.
USER 
USER A pending request may be withdrawn immediately at any time before final approval. Upon successful withdrawal, the withdrawal becomes effective immediately, all remaining approval processing for that request terminates, later approve/reject actions are prevented, and the concerned roles are informed.
USER 
USER After final approval, withdrawal is no longer available. Before the approved leave begins, the employee may request cancellation. Cancellation follows the same applicable hierarchy as the original leave and the approved leave is not edited in place. If cancellation is finally approved, the leave is marked Cancelled and any leave balance already deducted for the approval is restored or adjusted accordingly. If cancellation is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged. If revised leave is still required after cancellation, a fresh application is submitted.
USER 
USER Once leave has begun, pre-start cancellation no longer applies; change is handled through the applicable extension or resumption process.
USER 
USER ## 9. Extension
USER 
USER An employee already on ongoing EL, COL, or VL may request additional leave time. The extension is validated using the applicable eligibility, balance, substitute, calendar, and approval conditions; required substitute consent is obtained before approval routing continues; and the extension follows the applicable Unit Head and configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER When an extension is approved, only the incremental additional leave period is posted and the original approved leave remains the base. When an extension is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged.
USER 
USER ## 10. Resumption
USER 
USER Resumption is supported for EL, COL, and VL. After returning, the employee submits the actual resumption date and required resumption details through ELM for review by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER When the employee resumes on the approved end date, the Sanctioning Authority reviews the resumption and no adjustment is made to the approved leave period or leave balance. When the employee resumes earlier or later than the approved end date, ELM records the actual leave period after review, adjusts the leave balance accordingly, and synchronizes the relevant leave/attendance records.
USER 
USER ## 11. Inquiry and employee-accessible records
USER 
USER ELM provides employees with access to their current leave balances and leave-account/request history, including request status and recorded final decisions. ELM also provides an employee-accessible printable/exportable representation of a leave application.
USER 
USER ## 12. Administrative functions and calendars
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles maintain leave-policy parameters, the relevant hierarchy configuration, and leave records where authorized. Policy parameters do not require a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle; ELM uses the current configured policy and hierarchy values.
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles create, revise, and publish the holiday/RH calendar. Date/RH validation uses the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar until a replacement is published.
USER 
USER Vacation periods used for VL are supplied through the institute Academic Calendar, which is maintained outside ELM by the appropriate academic module/service and is assumed to be available to ELM.
USER 
USER Authorized Leave Administrators may retrospectively record leave already sanctioned through an authorized paper/offline process where ELM could not be used because of illness, connectivity, server unavailability, or another acceptable reason. The entry captures sanction/reference evidence, is validated for record consistency, and updates the relevant leave balance/history. This is record synchronization, not a second approval workflow.
USER 
USER ## 13. Event-triggered year-end processing
USER 
USER When the configured year-end boundary is reached, ELM applies the defined category outcomes: unused CL, RH, and SCL lapse; unused faculty VL is converted to EL; EL and COL carry forward according to policy. The resulting leave-account transactions are recorded and processing exceptions are reported. The year-end boundary is an event trigger, not an ELM actor.
USER 
USER ## 14. SLA
USER 
USER Actionable workflow tasks use configurable reminder and escalation thresholds. When a task is assigned, the assigned user is notified. If the task is still pending at the reminder threshold, the responsible user is reminded and the action is logged; no reminder is issued if the task is no longer pending. If the task remains pending past the escalation threshold, ELM notifies or escalates to the configured higher/responsible authority and logs the escalation; no escalation is issued if the task is no longer pending.
USER 
USER ## 15. Scope boundary
USER 
USER This ELM baseline limits leave categories to CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, VL, and Station Leave as defined above. Other leave types and low-level design/implementation details are outside this requirements baseline. The Academic Calendar is an external dependency for VL vacation-period data.
USER 
USER ## Appendix A — Actor, Role, and External Dependency Registry
USER 
USER | ID | Preferred term | Responsibility |
USER |---|---|---|
USER | ACT-01 | Employee | Faculty or Staff member applying for and managing own leave. |
USER | ACT-02 | Substitute / Responsibility Holder | Employee nominated to accept responsibility during the applicant’s absence. |
USER | ACT-03 | Unit Head | Head of Department/Section/Office; final authority for CL/RH or reviewer/recommender for higher-sanction leave. |
USER | ACT-04 | Deputy Registrar (Estt.) / Establishment Processing Role | Performs establishment-level verification/routing/record functions where configured. |
USER | ACT-05 | Dean | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-06 | Registrar | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-07 | Director | Highest competent authority; Director-specific self-sanction rule applies. |
USER | ACT-08 | Leave Administrator | Maintains authorized ELM configuration/records and records offline-sanctioned leave where authorized. |
USER | EXT-01 | Academic Calendar service/module | External source of published vacation periods used for VL applicability. |
USER | EVT-01 | Year-end boundary | Event trigger for EL-UC-020 year-end leave processing; not an actor. |
USER | EVT-02 | Configured SLA threshold reached | Event trigger for EL-UC-021 SLA threshold processing; not an actor. |
USER 
USER Faculty and Staff are employee categories. Unit Head, Substitute, Leave Administrator, Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director are roles/authorities. EXT-01 is an external information dependency rather than an ELM actor performing a leave workflow action. EVT-01 and EVT-02 are event triggers; they initiate event-triggered use cases but are not actors.
USER 
USER ## Appendix B — Controlled Glossary
USER 
USER - **Term:** Meaning
USER - **Employee:** Faculty or Staff member using ELM for own leave activity.
USER - **Applicant:** Employee whose leave request is being processed.
USER - **Unit Head:** Head of the applicant’s Department, Section, or Office.
USER - **Substitute / Responsibility Holder:** One eligible employee nominated to carry responsibility during the applicant’s absence.
USER - **Sanctioning Authority:** Competent authority with final approval/rejection power for the applicable routed request.
USER - **Station Leave:** Permission/details associated with leaving Jabalpur, captured inside the same leave application.
USER - **Withdrawal:** Immediate termination of a pending request before final approval, including termination of remaining approval processing.
USER - **Cancellation:** Pre-start reversal request for an approved leave, processed through the applicable hierarchy.
USER - **Extension:** Request for additional leave time while EL/COL/VL is ongoing.
USER - **Resumption:** Employee declaration of actual return to duty after EL/COL/VL, reviewed by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Early resumption:** Return before the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Late resumption:** Return after the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Offline-sanctioned leave:** Leave already authorized through a valid paper/offline route and later entered into ELM for synchronization with sanction/reference evidence.
USER - **SLA:** Configurable response-time expectation for actionable workflow tasks, with pending-only reminder and escalation.
USER ```

USER ../elm_specs_md/pilot_kit/chunks/problem_statement.md
USER ```
USER # Employee Leave Management (ELM)
USER 
USER Synchronized Problem Statement and Controlled Context
USER 
USER Status: FINAL SYNCHRONIZED BASELINE | Version 1.4
USER 
USER > Revision basis
USER This final synchronized baseline incorporates the 18 domain adjudications made after the Phase-2 materials, the coherent-CFR / atomic-AFR requirements arrangement, and the finalized UC/WF formalism. The historical SRS-EL-001…107 set remains the immutable as-administered student baseline and is preserved through the companion crosswalk.
USER 
USER ## 1. Context
USER 
USER Fusion is the institute ERP used to provide administrative, academic, student, and related services. Users may hold one or more roles, and access to ELM capabilities is controlled through role-based permissions.
USER 
USER ELM supports leave-related activities for institute employees. Employees are Faculty or Staff, each with a designation and an organizational unit such as a Department, Section, or Office. Each organizational unit has a Unit Head. Depending on leave type and hierarchy, the Unit Head either gives the final decision or reviews and records Recommended / Not Recommended before the request moves through the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER ## 2. Leave categories and Station Leave
USER 
USER ELM supports CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, and VL. Station Leave permission/details are captured within the same leave application when the employee will be outside Jabalpur; Station Leave is not a separate leave-request process.
USER 
USER ## 3. Entitlement, applicability, and carry-forward
USER 
USER CL: 8 days/year for each employee; first-half and second-half CL are supported; an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day; unused CL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER RH: 2 days/year for each employee; an RH request must correspond to an applicable Restricted Holiday in the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar; unused RH does not carry forward.
USER 
USER SCL: up to 15 days/year for each employee for a special purpose considered admissible by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility rule is required in this baseline; unused SCL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER EL: non-faculty employees receive 30 days/year directly; unused EL carries forward.
USER 
USER VL: faculty receive 60 VL associated with vacation periods supplied through the institute Academic Calendar. VL may be availed only during an applicable vacation period. At year end, all unused VL converts to EL at 2 VL : 1 EL, including fractional conversion. VL itself does not carry forward; converted EL is credited to the faculty member’s EL balance, may carry forward, and may later be availed.
USER 
USER COL: 20 days/year in this baseline for each eligible employee; COL is medical/balance-controlled leave, supporting medical evidence may be required where applicable, and unused COL carries forward.
USER 
USER ## 4. Leave-day counting
USER 
USER For EL, COL, and VL, leave is counted continuously across the approved leave interval. Intervening weekends and closed holidays within the interval count as leave days. This continuing-count rule does not apply to CL, SCL, or RH.
USER 
USER ## 5. Applying for leave and validation
USER 
USER The employee submits one leave application containing leave category, requested period, purpose/reason, and other required information. Station Leave details are included in the same application when applicable.
USER 
USER Before a new request is allowed to proceed, ELM validates the employee’s leave-category eligibility, available entitlement/balance, applicable leave-counting and calendar conditions, and impermissible overlap with the employee’s active pending or approved leave. First-half and second-half CL on the same date are not treated as overlapping when they occupy different halves.
USER 
USER Leave-policy parameters do not use a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle in this baseline. Validation uses the current configured leave-policy parameters. Date and RH validation use the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar.
USER 
USER ## 6. Substitute / responsibility coverage
USER 
USER When responsibility coverage is required, the applicant nominates one substitute. The substitute must be another employee, must not be the applicant, and must not have overlapping pending or approved leave during the responsibility period.
USER 
USER The nominated substitute may accept or decline the assignment. Required substitute consent must be accepted before approval routing continues. If the substitute declines, the applicant may nominate another eligible substitute or withdraw the pending request.
USER 
USER ## 7. Review, sanction, and decision communication
USER 
USER For CL and RH, including associated Station Leave, the request is routed to the applicant’s Unit Head and the Unit Head is the final authority.
USER 
USER For SCL, EL, COL, and VL, the Unit Head reviews the request and records Recommended or Not Recommended. Not Recommended is not a final rejection for these categories. The request then proceeds through the configured hierarchy to the appropriate final Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER The processing/sanctioning path is resolved from the applicant, organizational unit, designation/role, leave category, and current hierarchy configuration. Named roles may include Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director. An Establishment processing step occurs where configured. The Director’s own leave follows the Director-specific self-sanction rule.
USER 
USER On a final decision, the decision is recorded and the employee is notified. On final approval, the employee’s leave record/account is also updated. Intermediate decisions and state changes need not generate general notifications; the employee can inspect them through leave status/history.
USER 
USER ## 8. Pending request, withdrawal, and approved-leave cancellation
USER 
USER A submitted pending leave request is not modified in place. If a change is required before final approval, the employee may withdraw the pending request and submit a new application if needed.
USER 
USER A pending request may be withdrawn immediately at any time before final approval. Upon successful withdrawal, the withdrawal becomes effective immediately, all remaining approval processing for that request terminates, later approve/reject actions are prevented, and the concerned roles are informed.
USER 
USER After final approval, withdrawal is no longer available. Before the approved leave begins, the employee may request cancellation. Cancellation follows the same applicable hierarchy as the original leave and the approved leave is not edited in place. If cancellation is finally approved, the leave is marked Cancelled and any leave balance already deducted for the approval is restored or adjusted accordingly. If cancellation is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged. If revised leave is still required after cancellation, a fresh application is submitted.
USER 
USER Once leave has begun, pre-start cancellation no longer applies; change is handled through the applicable extension or resumption process.
USER 
USER ## 9. Extension
USER 
USER An employee already on ongoing EL, COL, or VL may request additional leave time. The extension is validated using the applicable eligibility, balance, substitute, calendar, and approval conditions; required substitute consent is obtained before approval routing continues; and the extension follows the applicable Unit Head and configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER When an extension is approved, only the incremental additional leave period is posted and the original approved leave remains the base. When an extension is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged.
USER 
USER ## 10. Resumption
USER 
USER Resumption is supported for EL, COL, and VL. After returning, the employee submits the actual resumption date and required resumption details through ELM for review by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER When the employee resumes on the approved end date, the Sanctioning Authority reviews the resumption and no adjustment is made to the approved leave period or leave balance. When the employee resumes earlier or later than the approved end date, ELM records the actual leave period after review, adjusts the leave balance accordingly, and synchronizes the relevant leave/attendance records.
USER 
USER ## 11. Inquiry and employee-accessible records
USER 
USER ELM provides employees with access to their current leave balances and leave-account/request history, including request status and recorded final decisions. ELM also provides an employee-accessible printable/exportable representation of a leave application.
USER 
USER ## 12. Administrative functions and calendars
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles maintain leave-policy parameters, the relevant hierarchy configuration, and leave records where authorized. Policy parameters do not require a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle; ELM uses the current configured policy and hierarchy values.
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles create, revise, and publish the holiday/RH calendar. Date/RH validation uses the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar until a replacement is published.
USER 
USER Vacation periods used for VL are supplied through the institute Academic Calendar, which is maintained outside ELM by the appropriate academic module/service and is assumed to be available to ELM.
USER 
USER Authorized Leave Administrators may retrospectively record leave already sanctioned through an authorized paper/offline process where ELM could not be used because of illness, connectivity, server unavailability, or another acceptable reason. The entry captures sanction/reference evidence, is validated for record consistency, and updates the relevant leave balance/history. This is record synchronization, not a second approval workflow.
USER 
USER ## 13. Event-triggered year-end processing
USER 
USER When the configured year-end boundary is reached, ELM applies the defined category outcomes: unused CL, RH, and SCL lapse; unused faculty VL is converted to EL; EL and COL carry forward according to policy. The resulting leave-account transactions are recorded and processing exceptions are reported. The year-end boundary is an event trigger, not an ELM actor.
USER 
USER ## 14. SLA
USER 
USER Actionable workflow tasks use configurable reminder and escalation thresholds. When a task is assigned, the assigned user is notified. If the task is still pending at the reminder threshold, the responsible user is reminded and the action is logged; no reminder is issued if the task is no longer pending. If the task remains pending past the escalation threshold, ELM notifies or escalates to the configured higher/responsible authority and logs the escalation; no escalation is issued if the task is no longer pending.
USER 
USER ## 15. Scope boundary
USER 
USER This ELM baseline limits leave categories to CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, VL, and Station Leave as defined above. Other leave types and low-level design/implementation details are outside this requirements baseline. The Academic Calendar is an external dependency for VL vacation-period data.
USER 
USER ## Appendix A — Actor, Role, and External Dependency Registry
USER 
USER | ID | Preferred term | Responsibility |
USER |---|---|---|
USER | ACT-01 | Employee | Faculty or Staff member applying for and managing own leave. |
USER | ACT-02 | Substitute / Responsibility Holder | Employee nominated to accept responsibility during the applicant’s absence. |
USER | ACT-03 | Unit Head | Head of Department/Section/Office; final authority for CL/RH or reviewer/recommender for higher-sanction leave. |
USER | ACT-04 | Deputy Registrar (Estt.) / Establishment Processing Role | Performs establishment-level verification/routing/record functions where configured. |
USER | ACT-05 | Dean | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-06 | Registrar | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-07 | Director | Highest competent authority; Director-specific self-sanction rule applies. |
USER | ACT-08 | Leave Administrator | Maintains authorized ELM configuration/records and records offline-sanctioned leave where authorized. |
USER | EXT-01 | Academic Calendar service/module | External source of published vacation periods used for VL applicability. |
USER | EVT-01 | Year-end boundary | Event trigger for EL-UC-020 year-end leave processing; not an actor. |
USER | EVT-02 | Configured SLA threshold reached | Event trigger for EL-UC-021 SLA threshold processing; not an actor. |
USER 
USER Faculty and Staff are employee categories. Unit Head, Substitute, Leave Administrator, Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director are roles/authorities. EXT-01 is an external information dependency rather than an ELM actor performing a leave workflow action. EVT-01 and EVT-02 are event triggers; they initiate event-triggered use cases but are not actors.
USER 
USER ## Appendix B — Controlled Glossary
USER 
USER - **Term:** Meaning
USER - **Employee:** Faculty or Staff member using ELM for own leave activity.
USER - **Applicant:** Employee whose leave request is being processed.
USER - **Unit Head:** Head of the applicant’s Department, Section, or Office.
USER - **Substitute / Responsibility Holder:** One eligible employee nominated to carry responsibility during the applicant’s absence.
USER - **Sanctioning Authority:** Competent authority with final approval/rejection power for the applicable routed request.
USER - **Station Leave:** Permission/details associated with leaving Jabalpur, captured inside the same leave application.
USER - **Withdrawal:** Immediate termination of a pending request before final approval, including termination of remaining approval processing.
USER - **Cancellation:** Pre-start reversal request for an approved leave, processed through the applicable hierarchy.
USER - **Extension:** Request for additional leave time while EL/COL/VL is ongoing.
USER - **Resumption:** Employee declaration of actual return to duty after EL/COL/VL, reviewed by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Early resumption:** Return before the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Late resumption:** Return after the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Offline-sanctioned leave:** Leave already authorized through a valid paper/offline route and later entered into ELM for synchronization with sanction/reference evidence.
USER - **SLA:** Configurable response-time expectation for actionable workflow tasks, with pending-only reminder and escalation.
USER ```

USER 
USER     `user_id` is the IAM user id. It is a plain integer and NOT a ForeignKey —

USER # ELM Business Rules — categories, entitlement, counting, overlap, substitute (BR-EL-001..015)
USER 
USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-001 — Supported leave-category eligibility
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Eligibility
USER - **Rule Statement:** A new leave request shall use only CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, or VL and shall satisfy the category-applicability conditions in the current configured ELM policy.
USER - **Applies To:** New leave validation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Requested category/application context
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Reject/prevent progression when category applicability is not satisfied.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-002–003
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-009
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Revised to remove published-policy-version semantics.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-002 — Casual Leave entitlement and lapse
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each employee has 8 CL days per year; unused CL lapses at year end and does not carry forward.
USER - **Applies To:** CL validation/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** CL account/request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce 8-day annual entitlement and no carry-forward.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-004, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-003 — Restricted Holiday entitlement and applicability
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Calendar
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each employee has 2 RH days per year; an RH request must correspond to an applicable Restricted Holiday in the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar; unused RH lapses at year end.
USER - **Applies To:** RH validation/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** RH request/account
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce entitlement, calendar applicability, and lapse.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-005, CFR-EL-025, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-015; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded/confirmed after adjudication.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-004 — Special Casual Leave entitlement and admissibility
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each employee may use up to 15 SCL days per calendar year for a special purpose considered admissible by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility criterion is required; unused SCL lapses at year end.
USER - **Applies To:** SCL validation/decision/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** SCL request/account
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce annual limit; leave purpose is judged by authority; lapse unused SCL.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-006, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-005; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Clarified: admissibility is authority judgment, not separate encoded rule.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-005 — Non-faculty Earned Leave credit and carry-forward
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each non-faculty employee shall receive 30 EL days per year and unused EL shall carry forward according to policy.
USER - **Applies To:** EL account/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Non-faculty EL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Credit 30/year and preserve unused EL across year boundary.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-007, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-006 — Faculty Vacation Leave entitlement and vacation-period applicability
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Calendar
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each faculty member shall have 60 VL associated with vacation periods supplied by the institute Academic Calendar, and VL may be availed only during an applicable vacation period.
USER - **Applies To:** VL validation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Faculty VL request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce 60 VL and external Academic Calendar vacation-period applicability.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-008
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Clarified external vacation-period source.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-007 — VL-to-EL year-end conversion
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Calculation / Year-end
USER - **Rule Statement:** At year end, all unused faculty VL shall convert to EL at 2 VL : 1 EL including fractional conversion, and VL itself shall not carry forward.
USER - **Applies To:** Year-end processing
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Unused faculty VL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Convert at 2:1 including fractions; leave no unused VL balance as VL.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-008, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active, consolidated statement.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-008 — Treatment of EL created by VL conversion
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Account
USER - **Rule Statement:** EL created by VL conversion shall be credited to the faculty member's EL balance, may carry forward, and may subsequently be availed.
USER - **Applies To:** Year-end/account use
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Converted EL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Credit and retain converted EL as usable EL.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-008, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-020; EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-009 — Commuted Leave entitlement and treatment
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Evidence
USER - **Rule Statement:** Eligible employees have 20 COL days/year in this baseline; COL is medical/balance-controlled, supporting medical evidence may be required where applicable, and unused COL carries forward.
USER - **Applies To:** COL validation/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** COL request/account
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce annual entitlement, balance/evidence conditions, and carry-forward.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-009, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Consolidated.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-010 — Half-day CL treatment
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Calculation
USER - **Rule Statement:** CL shall support first-half and second-half requests; an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day.
USER - **Applies To:** CL validation/accounting
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Half-day CL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Recognize halves and deduct 0.5 day when approved.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-004
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-011 — Continuous-day counting
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Counting
USER - **Rule Statement:** For EL, COL, and VL, intervening weekends and closed holidays inside the continuous approved interval count as leave days; this rule does not apply to CL, SCL, or RH.
USER - **Applies To:** Leave-day calculation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** EL/COL/VL and excluded categories
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Apply category-specific counting boundary exactly.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-003, CFR-EL-010
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-009
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-012 — Applicant non-overlap with half-day CL exception
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Validation
USER - **Rule Statement:** A new request shall not impermissibly overlap the employee's active pending or approved leave; first-half and second-half CL on the same date are non-overlapping when they occupy different halves.
USER - **Applies To:** New-request validation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Employee leave intervals
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Block impermissible overlap; permit different CL halves on same date.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-003
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Confirmed PS gap.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-013 — Station Leave coupling
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Scope / Information
USER - **Rule Statement:** Station Leave permission/details, when applicable, shall be captured within the same leave application and shall not create a separate leave-request process.
USER - **Applies To:** Leave application
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Outside-Jabalpur context
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Keep Station Leave inside associated leave application.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-002
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-014 — Substitute eligibility
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Eligibility
USER - **Rule Statement:** When responsibility coverage is required, exactly one substitute is nominated; the substitute must be another employee, must not be the applicant, and must have no overlapping pending or approved leave during the responsibility period.
USER - **Applies To:** Substitute nomination
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Nominated employee and responsibility period
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Reject an ineligible nominee.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-011–012
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-002, EL-UC-009, EL-UC-019
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded to include not-the-applicant condition.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-015 — Substitute consent gate and decline recovery
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Routing / Eligibility
USER - **Rule Statement:** Required approval routing shall not proceed until the nominated substitute accepts. A decline leaves the consent gate closed; the applicant may nominate another eligible substitute or withdraw the pending request where applicable.
USER - **Applies To:** Substitute response
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Pending request requiring responsibility coverage
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Accept releases routing gate; decline does not.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-012
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-002, EL-UC-019
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded with adjudicated recovery path.
USER ```

USER # ELM Use Cases — apply, substitute, review, sanction (EL-UC-001..005, 019)
USER 
USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-001 — Apply for Leave
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Academic Calendar service (for VL vacation-period data); Substitute interaction is handled by EL-UC-002 when required.
USER - **Goal / Description:** Create and submit a valid new leave application and place it into the applicable processing path.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee needs to request CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, or VL.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Employee is authenticated and authorized for own leave activity.
USER • Current configured leave policy/hierarchy is available; effective published holiday/RH calendar is available; Academic Calendar vacation-period data is available where VL is requested.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee starts a new leave application.
USER 
USER M2. Employee selects the leave category and requested period and enters purpose/reason and other required information.
USER 
USER M3. System captures Station Leave details in the same application when applicable.
USER 
USER M4. System validates category eligibility, entitlement/balance, counting/calendar conditions, and impermissible overlap.
USER 
USER M5. When responsibility coverage is required, Employee nominates one eligible substitute.
USER 
USER M6. Employee submits the application.
USER 
USER M7. If substitute consent is required, System places the request in Awaiting Substitute Response; otherwise it routes the request to Unit Head review. A Director-applicant follows the self-sanction path.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Validation fails: identify the failed condition and do not allow progression.
USER 
USER A2 Substitute nomination invalid: reject the nominee and allow another eligible nominee.
USER 
USER A3 After a substitute decline, the Employee may invoke EL-UC-019 to nominate a replacement or EL-UC-007 to withdraw.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A valid submitted request exists in the correct next processing state.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No invalid request is allowed to progress; previously entered data may remain available as implementation permits.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-001–015, BR-EL-020, BR-EL-030–031, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-002–003, CFR-EL-011, CFR-EL-015
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-002 — Respond to Substitute Request
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Substitute / Responsibility Holder
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee
USER - **Goal / Description:** Accept or decline a nominated responsibility assignment.
USER - **Trigger:** A substitute responsibility request is assigned to the nominated employee.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The nominee is an eligible substitute for the responsibility period.
USER • The associated leave/extension request is awaiting substitute response.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Substitute opens the assigned responsibility request.
USER 
USER M2. System presents the applicant, responsibility period, and relevant request context.
USER 
USER M3. Substitute selects Accept or Decline.
USER 
USER M4. System records the response.
USER 
USER M5. If Accept, the consent gate is released and the request proceeds to Unit Head review; if Decline, the request becomes Applicant Action Required.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If the request is no longer pending, System prevents a stale response.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** The substitute response is recorded and the request enters the outcome-appropriate state.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No routing occurs without a valid recorded response.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-014–015, BR-EL-035–036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-012, CFR-EL-030
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-003 — Review Leave-Related Request at Unit Head
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Unit Head
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee; Establishment / final Sanctioning Authority as downstream roles
USER - **Goal / Description:** Review a routed new-leave, cancellation, or extension request and record the decision/recommendation permitted by the applicable policy.
USER - **Trigger:** A request enters the Unit Head work queue.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The request has passed applicable submission/consent gates.
USER • The Unit Head is the resolved Unit Head for the applicant.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Unit Head opens the assigned request and reviews the request context and available supporting information.
USER 
USER M2. For a new CL/RH request, Unit Head records final Approve or Reject.
USER 
USER M3. For higher-sanction new leave, cancellation of higher-sanction leave, or EL/COL/VL extension, Unit Head records Recommended or Not Recommended.
USER 
USER M4. System records the outcome.
USER 
USER M5. A Unit-Head-final outcome completes the decision; a recommendation outcome continues through the configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Director-applicant cases do not use the normal Unit Head path.
USER 
USER A2 Not Recommended for higher-sanction contexts is not a final rejection.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** The Unit Head outcome is recorded and the request is either finally decided or advanced to the configured next stage.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No unauthorized or out-of-scope decision is recorded.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-016–020, BR-EL-023, BR-EL-025, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-013–016, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-004 — Verify and Route Higher-Sanction Request
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Deputy Registrar (Estt.) / Establishment Processing Role
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Unit Head; final Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Perform the configured Establishment-level verification/routing step and forward the request to the resolved competent authority.
USER - **Trigger:** A higher-sanction request reaches an Establishment step that is configured for its path.
USER - **Preconditions:** • An Establishment step is configured for this request context.
USER • The required upstream review/consent outcome has been recorded.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Establishment opens the routed work item.
USER 
USER M2. System presents the request and resolved routing context.
USER 
USER M3. Establishment performs the configured verification/routing checks.
USER 
USER M4. System resolves/validates the next competent authority using current hierarchy configuration.
USER 
USER M5. Establishment forwards the request to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If no Establishment step is configured, this use case is bypassed.
USER 
USER A2 If routing context is inconsistent or incomplete, do not forward until the inconsistency is resolved through authorized configuration/record handling.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** The request is assigned to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** The request is not forwarded to an unauthorized/unresolved authority.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-018–019, BR-EL-023, BR-EL-025, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-005 — Make Final Sanctioning Decision
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Sanctioning Authority (Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, Director, or other configured competent authority)
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee; Unit Head; Establishment where configured
USER - **Goal / Description:** Record the final approve/reject decision for a routed higher-sanction new-leave, cancellation, or extension request.
USER - **Trigger:** A request is assigned to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The actor is the resolved competent final authority for the request.
USER • Required upstream routing and consent gates have been completed.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Authority opens the assigned request and reviews the request, recommendation, and available evidence.
USER 
USER M2. Authority records Approve or Reject.
USER 
USER M3. System records the final decision and notifies the employee.
USER 
USER M4. For new-leave approval, System updates the leave record/account.
USER 
USER M5. For cancellation approval, System marks the leave Cancelled and restores/adjusts deducted balance; cancellation rejection preserves the original approval.
USER 
USER M6. For extension approval, System posts only the incremental additional period; extension rejection preserves the original approved leave.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 SCL purpose admissibility is decided by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility criterion is required.
USER 
USER A2 Director-as-applicant uses the Director-specific self-sanction path.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A final decision and the context-appropriate completion effects are recorded.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No final decision is recorded by an unauthorized authority.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-004, BR-EL-017–020, BR-EL-023–025, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-006, CFR-EL-014–016, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-019 — Nominate Replacement Substitute After Decline
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Substitute / Responsibility Holder
USER - **Goal / Description:** Recover from a substitute decline by nominating one replacement eligible substitute.
USER - **Trigger:** A nominated substitute declines and the request enters Applicant Action Required.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The associated request remains pending.
USER • Responsibility coverage is still required.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee chooses to replace the declined substitute.
USER 
USER M2. Employee nominates one replacement.
USER 
USER M3. System validates replacement eligibility.
USER 
USER M4. System assigns a fresh substitute request and places the leave/extension request in Awaiting Substitute Response.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Employee may instead withdraw the pending request where withdrawal is applicable.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A fresh substitute response is awaited for an eligible replacement.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** An ineligible replacement is not assigned.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-014–015, BR-EL-022, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-011–012, CFR-EL-018
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ## 3. Retired Use Case
USER 
USER - **Use Case:** EL-UC-006 — Modify Pending Request
USER - **Disposition:** RETIRED
USER - **Reason:** The adjudicated baseline prohibits in-place post-submission modification. Change before final approval is handled by immediate withdrawal followed, if needed, by a fresh application.
USER - **Replacement semantics:** CFR-EL-017, CFR-EL-018; BR-EL-034, BR-EL-022; EL-UC-007 and EL-UC-001.
USER 
USER ## 4. Event-Triggered Use Cases
USER ```
USER

USER # ELM Business Rules — review, routing, lifecycle, authorization (BR-EL-016..038)

USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)

USER ### BR-EL-016 — Unit Head final scope for CL/RH
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** For CL and RH, including associated Station Leave, the applicant's Unit Head is the final approve/reject authority except for the Director-specific self-sanction case.
USER - **Applies To:** New leave / same-hierarchy cancellation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** CL/RH request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Final decision is made at Unit Head unless Director exception applies.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-013, CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003, EL-UC-008
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-017 — Unit Head recommendation for higher-sanction leave
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authority / Routing
USER - **Rule Statement:** For SCL, EL, COL, and VL, the Unit Head records Recommended or Not Recommended; Not Recommended is not a final rejection and both outcomes continue through the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER - **Applies To:** Higher-sanction new leave/extension/cancellation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Higher-sanction request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Record recommendation and continue routing.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Clarified after adjudication.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-018 — Higher-sanction routing
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Routing
USER - **Rule Statement:** SCL, EL, COL, and VL requests requiring higher sanction shall proceed through the current configured hierarchy to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Applies To:** Higher-sanction contexts
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Routed request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Route to competent final authority using current configuration.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003–005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Revised to current configuration terminology.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-019 — Authority resolution and optional Establishment step
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Routing / Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** The processing/sanctioning path shall be resolved from applicant, organizational unit, designation/role, leave category, and current hierarchy configuration; an Establishment step and the roles Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director occur only where configured.
USER - **Applies To:** Routing resolution
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Higher-sanction request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Resolve and use only configured processing/authority steps.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003–005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Revised to remove effective-published-policy wording.

USER ### BR-EL-020 — Director self-sanction
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authority / Exception
USER - **Rule Statement:** When the Director is the applicant, the Director-specific self-sanction path replaces the normal Unit Head/higher-authority route.
USER - **Applies To:** Director-applicant leave
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Applicant role = Director
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Use Director self-sanction path.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-015
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-022 — Immediate withdrawal of pending request
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle
USER - **Rule Statement:** A pending request may be withdrawn at any time before final approval; withdrawal is immediate, terminates remaining approval processing, blocks later approve/reject actions, and informs concerned roles. After final approval, withdrawal is unavailable.
USER - **Applies To:** Pending request lifecycle
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Pending before final approval
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Apply immediate terminal withdrawal effects.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-018
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-007
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Consolidated and strengthened.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-023 — Cancellation of approved leave before start
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Routing
USER - **Rule Statement:** An approved leave may be cancelled only before its start date; cancellation uses the same applicable hierarchy, does not edit the approved leave in place, approval marks the leave Cancelled and restores/adjusts already deducted balance, while rejection leaves the original approval unchanged.
USER - **Applies To:** Approved-leave cancellation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Approved and not started
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce pre-start boundary and context-specific final effects.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-008, EL-UC-003–005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-02
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded with adjudicated outcome effects.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-024 — Fresh application after cancellation
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle
USER - **Rule Statement:** If revised leave is still required after cancellation, a fresh leave application shall be submitted rather than editing the approved leave in place.
USER - **Applies To:** Approved-leave change
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Successful cancellation followed by revised need
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Require fresh application.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-008, EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-02
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-025 — Extension of ongoing EL/COL/VL
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Calculation
USER - **Rule Statement:** Extension is available only for ongoing EL/COL/VL; it uses applicable eligibility, balance, substitute, calendar and sanction rules; approval posts only the incremental additional period while rejection preserves the original approved leave.
USER - **Applies To:** Ongoing leave extension
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Ongoing EL/COL/VL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Validate/rout appropriately and apply incremental-only approval effect.

USER ### BR-EL-027 — Resumption applicability and review authority
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** Formal resumption review applies to EL, COL, and VL; the employee submits actual resumption information and the applicable Sanctioning Authority reviews it.
USER - **Applies To:** Resumption
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Return from EL/COL/VL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Route submitted resumption to applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-021

USER ### BR-EL-028 — Normal, early, and late resumption outcome
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Account
USER - **Rule Statement:** If actual resumption equals the approved end date, review causes no adjustment to approved leave period or balance. If actual resumption is earlier or later, after review ELM records the actual leave period, adjusts leave balance accordingly, and synchronizes relevant leave/attendance records.
USER - **Applies To:** Resumption review
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Compare actual vs approved end date
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Apply no-adjustment or adjusted synchronization branch.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-021
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-011

USER ### BR-EL-029 — Offline-sanctioned leave synchronization
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Record integrity
USER - **Rule Statement:** Only leave already sanctioned through an authorized offline/paper process may be retrospectively recorded; sanction/reference evidence shall be captured, record consistency validated, relevant balance/history synchronized, and no second approval workflow initiated.
USER - **Applies To:** Offline leave entry
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Already-sanctioned leave + acceptable ELM-unavailable context

USER - **Rule Type:** Threshold / Notification

USER ### BR-EL-033 — SLA escalation on still-pending task
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Threshold / Escalation
USER - **Rule Statement:** When an SLA-controlled actionable task remains pending past its configured escalation threshold, the system shall perform the configured escalation action: notify the configured higher/responsible authority and, where the escalation configuration changes routing/responsibility, apply that routing/responsibility change; the escalation action shall be logged. If the task is no longer pending, no escalation shall be issued.

USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Task past escalation threshold

ASSISTANT """
ASSISTANT Service layer for applying, substitute consent and decisions.
ASSISTANT """
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT from django.db import transaction
ASSISTANT from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
ASSISTANT from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
ASSISTANT from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
ASSISTANT from .domain.state_machine import Transition
ASSISTANT from .domain.policy import get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories, get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories

ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT @transaction.atomic
ASSISTANT def apply_leave(principal_id: int, *, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, reason: str = None, substitute_id: int = None) -> str:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Apply for leave.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     :param principal_id: The id of the acting person.
ASSISTANT     :param leave_category: The leave category.
ASSISTANT     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
ASSISTANT     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
ASSISTANT     :param reason: The reason for the leave.
ASSISTANT     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute, if applicable.
ASSISTANT     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         principal = Principal(user_id=principal_id)
ASSISTANT         if not get_cl_applicable_categories(principal, leave_category):
ASSISTANT             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' is not applicable for principal '{principal}'.")

USER # ELM Use Cases — withdraw, cancel, extend, resume, inquiry (EL-UC-007..013)
USER 
USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-007 — Withdraw Pending Request
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Concerned processing/sanctioning roles
USER - **Goal / Description:** Immediately terminate a pending request before final approval.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee chooses to withdraw a request that is still pending before final approval.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The request is pending and has not received final approval.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee selects the pending request and confirms withdrawal.
USER 
USER M2. System validates that final approval has not occurred.
USER 
USER M3. System makes the withdrawal effective immediately.
USER 
USER M4. System terminates all remaining approval processing, prevents later approve/reject actions, and notifies concerned roles.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If final approval already exists, withdrawal is unavailable; approved-leave cancellation applies where its preconditions hold.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Request is Withdrawn and no remaining approval action can be taken.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No withdrawal is applied to an already finally approved request.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-022, BR-EL-034, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-017–018
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03 (only when the extension request itself is pending and withdrawal is applicable)
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-008 — Request Cancellation of Approved Leave
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Unit Head; Establishment where configured; final Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Request pre-start cancellation of an approved leave without editing the approved leave in place.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee needs to reverse an approved leave before its approved start date.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Leave is finally Approved.
USER • Approved start date has not yet occurred.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee selects the approved leave and requests cancellation.
USER 
USER M2. System validates that the leave has not started and that withdrawal is no longer the applicable mechanism.
USER 
USER M3. System creates the cancellation request without editing the approved leave.
USER 
USER M4. System routes the cancellation to the Unit Head and then through the same applicable hierarchy as the original leave.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If leave has started, cancellation is unavailable; extension/resumption applies as relevant.
USER 
USER A2 If cancellation is finally rejected, original approved leave remains unchanged.
USER 
USER A3 If cancellation is approved and revised leave is still needed, Employee submits a fresh leave application.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A valid cancellation request enters the applicable decision path.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No pre-start cancellation request is created outside its eligibility boundary.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-023–024, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-02
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-009 — Request Extension of Ongoing Leave
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Substitute; Unit Head; Establishment where configured; final Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Request additional leave time while ongoing EL, COL, or VL is being availed.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee on ongoing EL/COL/VL needs additional leave time.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Original leave is ongoing and is EL, COL, or VL.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee enters the requested additional period and required information.
USER 
USER M2. System validates eligibility, balance, calendar/counting conditions and other applicable rules.
USER 
USER M3. When responsibility coverage is required, Employee nominates an eligible substitute and obtains consent through EL-UC-002.
USER 
USER M4. System creates the extension request and routes it to Unit Head review and the configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Validation failure prevents the extension request from progressing.
USER 
USER A2 Substitute decline invokes replacement handling through EL-UC-019; no approval routing occurs before required acceptance.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A valid extension request enters the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** Original approved leave remains unaffected by an invalid/unsubmitted extension request.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005–015, BR-EL-017–019, BR-EL-025, BR-EL-030–031, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-010 — Submit Resumption of Duty
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Applicable Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Submit actual return-to-duty information after EL, COL, or VL for review.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee returns to duty after EL/COL/VL.
USER - **Preconditions:** • An EL/COL/VL leave record exists for the employee.
USER • Employee has returned and can state the actual resumption date.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee opens the relevant leave record.
USER 
USER M2. Employee enters actual resumption date and required resumption details.
USER 
USER M3. System records the resumption submission and routes it to the applicable Sanctioning Authority for review.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Resumption is outside this baseline for CL/RH/SCL as a formal reviewed process.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A resumption submission exists in Resumption Review Pending.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No review submission is created if required information is missing/invalid.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-027, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-04
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-011 — Review Resumption
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Applicable Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee; leave/attendance records
USER - **Goal / Description:** Review the submitted resumption and apply only the adjustments required by the actual return date.
USER - **Trigger:** A resumption submission is assigned for review.
USER - **Preconditions:** • A valid resumption submission exists for EL/COL/VL.
USER • Actor is the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Authority reviews the submitted actual resumption date/details.
USER 
USER M2. System compares actual resumption date with approved end date.
USER 
USER M3. If dates match, Authority records review completion; no approved leave-period or balance adjustment is made.
USER 
USER M4. If actual date is earlier or later, Authority confirms the review outcome and System records actual leave period, adjusts balance accordingly, and synchronizes relevant leave/attendance records.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 No generic 'close leave' state transition is asserted for normal resumption in this baseline.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Resumption review is recorded; early/late return produces the required data/balance synchronization.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No balance/period adjustment is applied before required review.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-027–028, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-04
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-012 — View Leave Balances and History
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** None
USER - **Goal / Description:** Inspect own current leave balances and leave-account/request history, including request status and final decisions.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee wants to inspect current balances or historical/request status information.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Employee is authenticated and authorized to access own leave information.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee opens leave balance/history inquiry.
USER 
USER M2. System displays current leave balances.
USER 
USER M3. System displays accessible leave-account/request history including current request status and recorded final decisions.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Requested balance/history information is displayed.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No other employee's protected leave information is exposed.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-016, CFR-EL-022
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-013 — Obtain Printable/Exportable Leave Application
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** None
USER - **Goal / Description:** Obtain a printable or exportable representation of an accessible leave application.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee needs a printable/exportable copy of an accessible leave application.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Employee is authorized to access the application.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee selects an accessible leave application.
USER 
USER M2. Employee requests print/export representation.
USER 
USER M3. System produces the representation for the selected application.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Printable/exportable representation is available.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No representation is produced for an application the employee is not authorized to access.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-023
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER ```

USER Service layer for applying, substitute consent and decisions.
USER """
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER from .domain.policy import get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories, get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def apply_leave(principal_id: int, *, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, reason: str = None, substitute_id: int = None) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Apply for leave.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the acting person.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param reason: The reason for the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute, if applicable.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         principal = Principal(user_id=principal_id)
USER         if not get_cl_applicable_categories(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' is not applicable for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_entitlement(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Principal '{principal}' does not have entitlement for leave category '{leave_category}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_lapse(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' does not lapse for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         transition = Transition(principal, leave_category, "apply", start_date, end_date, reason)
USER         transition.apply()
USER         
USER         if substitute_id:
USER             respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent."
USER         else:
USER             unit_head_review(principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting Unit Head review."
USER     except ValidationError as e:
USER         return str(e)
