USER modules/leave/services/applications.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Service layer for applying, substitute consent and decisions.
USER """
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER from .domain.policy import get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories, get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def apply_leave(principal_id: int, *, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, reason: str = None, substitute_id: int = None) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Apply for leave.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the acting person.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param reason: The reason for the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute, if applicable.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         principal = Principal(user_id=principal_id)
USER         if not get_cl_applicable_categories(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' is not applicable for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_entitlement(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Principal '{principal}' does not have entitlement for leave category '{leave_category}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_lapse(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' does not lapse for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         transition = Transition(principal, leave_category, "apply", start_date, end_date, reason)
USER         transition.apply()
USER         
USER         if substitute_id:
USER             respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent."
USER         else:
USER             unit_head_review(principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting Unit Head review."
USER     except ValidationError as e:
USER         return str(e)
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id: int, principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, action: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Respond to a substitute request.
USER 
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute.
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param action: The action to take ('accept' or 'decline').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if action not in ["accept", "decline"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid action '{action}'. Action must be 'accept' or 'decline'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, action)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     if action == "accept":
USER         return "Substitute consent given. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER     else:
USER         return "Substitute declined. Request routed to Unit Head for final decision."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def nominate_replacement_substitute(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, substitute_id: int) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Nominate a replacement substitute.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the replacement substitute.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, "nominate_replacement_substitute")
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Replacement substitute '{substitute_id}' nominated for principal '{principal_id}'. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Unit Head makes a final decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "unit_head_review", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Unit Head made a final decision: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def establishment_verify_and_route(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Establishment verifies and routes a higher-sanction request.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "establishment_verify_and_route", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Establishment verified and routed the request. Recommendation: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, decision: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Make a final sanctioning decision.
USER

USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.

USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.

USER     """

USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}'. Decision must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "final_sanction_decision", start_date, end_date, decision)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Final sanctioning decision made: '{decision}' for principal '{principal_id}'."

USER fusion_auth/permissions.py
USER ```
USER """Two gates on every request, both server-side.
USER 
USER     HasModuleGrant   is this module yours at all?   (coarse — drives the sidebar)
USER     HasPermission    may you do this action?        (fine)
USER 
USER Both must pass. The default is deny: a view that declares nothing gets
USER IsAuthenticatedPrincipal and nothing more, so forgetting to think about
USER authorization fails closed rather than open.
USER """
USER from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
USER 
USER 
USER class IsAuthenticatedPrincipal(BasePermission):
USER     message = "Authentication required."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         return getattr(request, "principal", None) is not None
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasModuleGrant(BasePermission):
USER     module_code = ""
USER     message = "This module is not available to your role."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         return p is not None and p.has_module(self.module_code)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasModuleGrant(module_code: str):                       # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasModuleGrant_" + module_code,
USER                 (_HasModuleGrant,), {"module_code": module_code})
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasPermission(BasePermission):
USER     codes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
USER     require_all = False
USER     message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         if p is None:
USER             return False
USER         if self.require_all:
USER             return all(p.has_permission(c) for c in self.codes)
USER         return p.has_any_permission(*self.codes)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasPermission(*codes: str, require_all: bool = False):  # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasPermission_" + "_".join(codes).replace(".", "_"),
USER                 (_HasPermission,), {"codes": codes, "require_all": require_all})
USER ```

USER modules/leave/services/applications.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Service layer for applying, substitute consent and decisions.
USER """
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER from .domain.policy import get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories, get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def apply_leave(principal_id: int, *, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, reason: str = None, substitute_id: int = None) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Apply for leave.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the acting person.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param reason: The reason for the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute, if applicable.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         principal = Principal(user_id=principal_id)
USER         if not get_cl_applicable_categories(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' is not applicable for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_entitlement(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Principal '{principal}' does not have entitlement for leave category '{leave_category}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_lapse(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' does not lapse for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         transition = Transition(principal, leave_category, "apply", start_date, end_date, reason)
USER         transition.apply()
USER         
USER         if substitute_id:
USER             respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent."
USER         else:
USER             unit_head_review(principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting Unit Head review."
USER     except ValidationError as e:
USER         return str(e)
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id: int, principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, action: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Respond to a substitute request.
USER 
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute.
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param action: The action to take ('accept' or 'decline').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if action not in ["accept", "decline"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid action '{action}'. Action must be 'accept' or 'decline'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, action)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     if action == "accept":
USER         return "Substitute consent given. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER     else:
USER         return "Substitute declined. Request routed to Unit Head for final decision."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def nominate_replacement_substitute(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, substitute_id: int) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Nominate a replacement substitute.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the replacement substitute.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, "nominate_replacement_substitute")
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Replacement substitute '{substitute_id}' nominated for principal '{principal_id}'. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Unit Head makes a final decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "unit_head_review", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Unit Head made a final decision: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def establishment_verify_and_route(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Establishment verifies and routes a higher-sanction request.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "establishment_verify_and_route", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Establishment verified and routed the request. Recommendation: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, decision: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Make a final sanctioning decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param decision: The decision ('approve' or 'reject').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if decision not in ["approve", "reject"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}'. Decision must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "final_sanction_decision", start_date, end_date, decision)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Final sanctioning decision made: '{decision}' for principal '{principal_id}'."

USER fusion_auth/permissions.py
USER ```
USER """Two gates on every request, both server-side.
USER 
USER     HasModuleGrant   is this module yours at all?   (coarse — drives the sidebar)
USER     HasPermission    may you do this action?        (fine)
USER 
USER Both must pass. The default is deny: a view that declares nothing gets
USER IsAuthenticatedPrincipal and nothing more, so forgetting to think about
USER authorization fails closed rather than open.
USER """
USER from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
USER 
USER 
USER class IsAuthenticatedPrincipal(BasePermission):
USER     message = "Authentication required."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         return getattr(request, "principal", None) is not None
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasModuleGrant(BasePermission):
USER     module_code = ""
USER     message = "This module is not available to your role."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         return p is not None and p.has_module(self.module_code)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasModuleGrant(module_code: str):                       # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasModuleGrant_" + module_code,
USER                 (_HasModuleGrant,), {"module_code": module_code})
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasPermission(BasePermission):
USER     codes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
USER     require_all = False
USER     message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         if p is None:
USER             return False
USER         if self.require_all:
USER             return all(p.has_permission(c) for c in self.codes)
USER         return p.has_any_permission(*self.codes)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasPermission(*codes: str, require_all: bool = False):  # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasPermission_" + "_".join(codes).replace(".", "_"),
USER                 (_HasPermission,), {"codes": codes, "require_all": require_all})
USER ```

USER - There is no user table in this repository. The logged-in person is request.principal (fusion_auth.principal.Principal: user_id, username, kind, roles, permissions). Store people as integer ids (models.IntegerField), never as a ForeignKey to a user model.

USER modules/leave/services/applications.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Service layer for applying, substitute consent and decisions.
USER """
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER from .domain.policy import get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories, get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def apply_leave(principal_id: int, *, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, reason: str = None, substitute_id: int = None) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Apply for leave.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the acting person.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param reason: The reason for the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute, if applicable.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         principal = Principal(user_id=principal_id)
USER         if not get_cl_applicable_categories(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' is not applicable for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_entitlement(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Principal '{principal}' does not have entitlement for leave category '{leave_category}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_lapse(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' does not lapse for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         transition = Transition(principal, leave_category, "apply", start_date, end_date, reason)
USER         transition.apply()
USER         
USER         if substitute_id:
USER             respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent."
USER         else:
USER             unit_head_review(principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting Unit Head review."
USER     except ValidationError as e:
USER         return str(e)
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id: int, principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, action: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Respond to a substitute request.
USER 
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute.
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param action: The action to take ('accept' or 'decline').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if action not in ["accept", "decline"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid action '{action}'. Action must be 'accept' or 'decline'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, action)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     if action == "accept":
USER         return "Substitute consent given. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER     else:
USER         return "Substitute declined. Request routed to Unit Head for final decision."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def nominate_replacement_substitute(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, substitute_id: int) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Nominate a replacement substitute.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the replacement substitute.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, "nominate_replacement_substitute")
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Replacement substitute '{substitute_id}' nominated for principal '{principal_id}'. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Unit Head makes a final decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "unit_head_review", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Unit Head made a final decision: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def establishment_verify_and_route(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Establishment verifies and routes a higher-sanction request.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "establishment_verify_and_route", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Establishment verified and routed the request. Recommendation: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, decision: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Make a final sanctioning decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param decision: The decision ('approve' or 'reject').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if decision not in ["approve", "reject"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}'. Decision must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "final_sanction_decision", start_date, end_date, decision)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Final sanctioning decision made: '{decision}' for principal '{principal_id}'."

USER fusion_auth/permissions.py
USER ```
USER """Two gates on every request, both server-side.
USER 
USER     HasModuleGrant   is this module yours at all?   (coarse — drives the sidebar)
USER     HasPermission    may you do this action?        (fine)
USER 
USER Both must pass. The default is deny: a view that declares nothing gets
USER IsAuthenticatedPrincipal and nothing more, so forgetting to think about
USER authorization fails closed rather than open.
USER """
USER from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
USER 
USER 
USER class IsAuthenticatedPrincipal(BasePermission):
USER     message = "Authentication required."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         return getattr(request, "principal", None) is not None
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasModuleGrant(BasePermission):
USER     module_code = ""
USER     message = "This module is not available to your role."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         return p is not None and p.has_module(self.module_code)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasModuleGrant(module_code: str):                       # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasModuleGrant_" + module_code,
USER                 (_HasModuleGrant,), {"module_code": module_code})
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasPermission(BasePermission):
USER     codes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
USER     require_all = False
USER     message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         if p is None:
USER             return False
USER         if self.require_all:
USER             return all(p.has_permission(c) for c in self.codes)
USER         return p.has_any_permission(*self.codes)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasPermission(*codes: str, require_all: bool = False):  # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasPermission_" + "_".join(codes).replace(".", "_"),
USER                 (_HasPermission,), {"codes": codes, "require_all": require_all})
USER ```

USER 
USER Repository rules you must follow:
USER - There is no user table in this repository. The logged-in person is request.principal (fusion_auth.principal.Principal: user_id, username, kind, roles, permissions). Store people as integer ids (models.IntegerField), never as a ForeignKey to a user model.
USER - A module copies the shape of modules/placement: apps.py, registry.py, models.py, domain/ (pure Python, no Django imports), services/ (all writes), api/ (serializers, views, urls).

USER modules/leave/services/applications.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Service layer for applying, substitute consent and decisions.
USER """
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER from .domain.policy import get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories, get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def apply_leave(principal_id: int, *, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, reason: str = None, substitute_id: int = None) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Apply for leave.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the acting person.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param reason: The reason for the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute, if applicable.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         principal = Principal(user_id=principal_id)
USER         if not get_cl_applicable_categories(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' is not applicable for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_entitlement(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Principal '{principal}' does not have entitlement for leave category '{leave_category}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_lapse(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' does not lapse for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         transition = Transition(principal, leave_category, "apply", start_date, end_date, reason)
USER         transition.apply()
USER         
USER         if substitute_id:
USER             respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent."
USER         else:
USER             unit_head_review(principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting Unit Head review."
USER     except ValidationError as e:
USER         return str(e)
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id: int, principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, action: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Respond to a substitute request.
USER 
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute.
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param action: The action to take ('accept' or 'decline').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if action not in ["accept", "decline"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid action '{action}'. Action must be 'accept' or 'decline'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, action)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     if action == "accept":
USER         return "Substitute consent given. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER     else:
USER         return "Substitute declined. Request routed to Unit Head for final decision."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def nominate_replacement_substitute(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, substitute_id: int) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Nominate a replacement substitute.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the replacement substitute.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, "nominate_replacement_substitute")
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Replacement substitute '{substitute_id}' nominated for principal '{principal_id}'. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Unit Head makes a final decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "unit_head_review", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Unit Head made a final decision: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def establishment_verify_and_route(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Establishment verifies and routes a higher-sanction request.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "establishment_verify_and_route", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Establishment verified and routed the request. Recommendation: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, decision: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Make a final sanctioning decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param decision: The decision ('approve' or 'reject').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if decision not in ["approve", "reject"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}'. Decision must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "final_sanction_decision", start_date, end_date, decision)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Final sanctioning decision made: '{decision}' for principal '{principal_id}'."

USER fusion_auth/permissions.py
USER ```
USER """Two gates on every request, both server-side.
USER 
USER     HasModuleGrant   is this module yours at all?   (coarse — drives the sidebar)
USER     HasPermission    may you do this action?        (fine)
USER 
USER Both must pass. The default is deny: a view that declares nothing gets
USER IsAuthenticatedPrincipal and nothing more, so forgetting to think about
USER authorization fails closed rather than open.
USER """
USER from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
USER 
USER 
USER class IsAuthenticatedPrincipal(BasePermission):
USER     message = "Authentication required."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         return getattr(request, "principal", None) is not None
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasModuleGrant(BasePermission):
USER     module_code = ""
USER     message = "This module is not available to your role."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         return p is not None and p.has_module(self.module_code)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasModuleGrant(module_code: str):                       # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasModuleGrant_" + module_code,
USER                 (_HasModuleGrant,), {"module_code": module_code})
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasPermission(BasePermission):
USER     codes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
USER     require_all = False
USER     message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         if p is None:
USER             return False
USER         if self.require_all:
USER             return all(p.has_permission(c) for c in self.codes)
USER         return p.has_any_permission(*self.codes)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasPermission(*codes: str, require_all: bool = False):  # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasPermission_" + "_".join(codes).replace(".", "_"),
USER                 (_HasPermission,), {"codes": codes, "require_all": require_all})
USER ```

USER identity, RBAC and directory data all come from Fusion_System_Administrator

USER import os

USER 
USER BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
USER 
USER 
USER def _load_env_file(path):
USER     """Fill in from .env whatever the environment has not already set.
USER 
USER     Local development only in practice: production passes real variables
USER     through systemd, and setdefault means those always win. Without this,
USER     `cp .env.example .env` does nothing and every entry point depends on one
USER     shell's exports.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         lines = path.read_text().splitlines()
USER     except OSError:
USER         return
USER     for line in lines:
USER         line = line.strip()
USER         if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
USER             continue
USER         name, _, value = line.partition("=")
USER         os.environ.setdefault(name.strip(), value.strip().strip('"').strip("'"))
USER 
USER 
USER _load_env_file(BASE_DIR / ".env")
USER 
USER 
USER def env(name, default=None):
USER     v = os.environ.get(name)
USER     return default if v is None or v.strip() == "" else v.strip()
USER 
USER 
USER def env_bool(name, default=False):
USER     raw = env(name)
USER     return default if raw is None else raw.lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
USER 
USER 
USER def env_int(name, default=None):
USER     raw = env(name)
USER     try:
USER         return int(raw) if raw is not None else default
USER     except ValueError:
USER         return default
USER 
USER 
USER def env_list(name, default=None):
USER     raw = env(name)
USER     if raw is None:
USER         return list(default or [])
USER     return [x.strip() for x in raw.split(",") if x.strip()]
USER

USER client/src/lib/http.ts
USER ```
USER /** One axios instance for the whole app, with the interceptors the legacy
USER  *  academic SPA never had. */
USER import axios from "axios";
USER 
USER export const http = axios.create({
USER   baseURL: "/api/v1",
USER   withCredentials: true,          // the session cookie, never a JS-readable token
USER   timeout: 15000,
USER });
USER 
USER /** Paired with the session cookie. Held in memory rather than a readable
USER  *  cookie, which the SPA could not read cross-origin in development. */
USER let csrfToken = "";
USER export function setCsrfToken(value: string) {
USER   csrfToken = value ?? "";
USER }
USER 
USER const UNSAFE = new Set(["post", "put", "patch", "delete"]);
USER 
USER http.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
USER   if (csrfToken && UNSAFE.has((config.method ?? "get").toLowerCase())) {
USER     config.headers.set("X-CSRF-Token", csrfToken);
USER   }
USER   return config;
USER });
USER 
USER let onUnauthorized: (() => void) | null = null;
USER export function setUnauthorizedHandler(fn: () => void) {
USER   onUnauthorized = fn;
USER }
USER 
USER http.interceptors.response.use(
USER   (r) => r,
USER   (error) => {
USER     if (error.response?.status === 401) onUnauthorized?.();
USER     return Promise.reject(error);
USER   },
USER );
USER 
USER /** Pull a usable message out of the platform's error envelope. */
USER export function errorMessage(e: unknown): string {
USER   const env = (e as { response?: { data?: { error?: {
USER     message?: string; details?: { message?: string }[] } } } })
USER     ?.response?.data?.error;
USER   if (!env) return "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
USER   if (env.details?.length) {
USER     return env.details.map((d) => d.message).filter(Boolean).join(" ") ||
USER       env.message || "Request failed.";
USER   }
USER   return env.message ?? "Request failed.";
USER }
USER 
USER /** A `responseType: "blob"` request delivers the *error* body as a Blob too,
USER  *  which hides the envelope from errorMessage. Read it back before rethrowing. */
USER export async function readBlobError(e: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
USER   const res = (e as { response?: { data?: unknown } })?.response;
USER   if (res?.data instanceof Blob) {
USER     try {
USER       res.data = JSON.parse(await res.data.text());
USER     } catch {
USER       res.data = undefined;             // an HTML page, not our envelope
USER     }
USER   }
USER   return e;
USER }
USER 
USER export function errorStatus(e: unknown): number | undefined {
USER   return (e as { response?: { status?: number } })?.response?.status;
USER }
USER 
USER export function requestId(e: unknown): string | undefined {
USER   return (e as { response?: { data?: { error?: { request_id?: string } } } })
USER     ?.response?.data?.error?.request_id;
USER }
USER ```

USER /** Frontend module registry.
USER  *
USER  *  `code` MUST match accesscontrol_module.code on the server. An ungranted
USER  *  module gets no route at all, so deep-linking it 404s rather than rendering
USER  *  blank. `load` must be a STATIC import() literal or Vite cannot split it. */

USER 
USER export interface ModuleManifest {
USER   code: string;
USER   basePath: string;
USER   load: () => Promise<{ routes: RouteObject[] }>;
USER }
USER

USER import { useState } from "react";
USER import {
USER   ActionIcon, Button, Card, Container, Group, Stack, Text,
USER } from "@mantine/core";
USER import { notifications } from "@mantine/notifications";
USER import { FaHistory, FaUserCheck } from "react-icons/fa";
USER 
USER import { errorMessage } from "../../../lib/http";
USER import { DataTable } from "../../../ui/components/DataTable";
USER import { ErrorState } from "../../../ui/components/ErrorState";
USER import { PageHeader } from "../../../ui/components/PageHeader";
USER import { StatusBadge } from "../../../ui/components/StatusBadge";
USER import { CpiBadge } from "../components/CpiBadge";
USER import { HistoryDrawer } from "../components/HistoryDrawer";
USER import { useApplications, useTransition } from "../api/hooks";
USER import type { Application } from "../api/types";
USER 
USER export default function MyApplicationsPage() {
USER   const { data, isPending, error } = useApplications();
USER   const transition = useTransition();
USER   const [historyFor, setHistoryFor] = useState<number | null>(null);
USER 
USER   if (error) return <Container size="xl"><ErrorState error={error} /></Container>;
USER 
USER   function withdraw(app: Application) {
USER     transition.mutate(
USER       { id: app.id, to_status: "withdrawn", reason: "Withdrawn by the student" },
USER       {
USER         onSuccess: () => notifications.show({
USER           color: "gray", title: "Withdrawn",
USER           message: `Your application to ${app.posting.title} has been withdrawn.`,
USER         }),
USER         onError: (e) => notifications.show({
USER           color: "red", title: "Could not withdraw", message: errorMessage(e),
USER         }),
USER       },
USER     );
USER   }
USER 
USER   return (
USER     <Container size="xl">
USER       <PageHeader
USER         title="My Applications"
USER         subtitle="Every application you have submitted, and where it stands"
USER       />
USER       <Card padding="lg">
USER         <DataTable<Application>
USER           rows={(data?.results ?? []) as Application[]}
USER           loading={isPending}
USER           rowKey={(r) => r.id}
USER           minWidth={900}
USER           columns={[
USER             {
USER               key: "posting", header: "Role",
USER               render: (r) => (
USER                 <Stack gap={0}>
USER                   <Text fw={600}>{r.posting?.title}</Text>
USER                   <Text size="xs" c="dimmed">{r.posting?.company?.name}</Text>
USER                 </Stack>
USER               ),
USER             },
USER             {
USER               key: "cpi_at_apply", header: "CPI at apply",
USER               // The frozen value: what the decision was made on, not today's CPI.
USER               render: (r) => (
USER                 <CpiBadge
USER                   cpi={r.cpi_at_apply}
USER                   standing={{
USER                     semester: r.semester_at_apply, semester_type: null,
USER                     declared_seq: null, synced_at: null, computed_by: null,
USER                   }}
USER                 />
USER               ),
USER             },
USER             { key: "applied_at", header: "Applied",
USER               render: (r) => r.applied_at
USER                 ? new Date(r.applied_at).toLocaleDateString() : "—" },
USER             { key: "status", header: "Status",
USER               render: (r) => <StatusBadge status={r.status} /> },
USER             {
USER               key: "actions", header: "", align: "right",
USER               render: (r) => (
USER                 <Group justify="flex-end" gap="xs">
USER                   {/* "Why is my application at this status?" answered without
USER                       a support request. */}
USER                   <ActionIcon
USER                     variant="subtle" color="gray" aria-label="History"
USER                     onClick={() => setHistoryFor(r.id)}
USER                   >
USER                     <FaHistory size={12} />
USER                   </ActionIcon>
USER                   {r.allowed_transitions?.includes("withdrawn") && (
USER                     <Button
USER                       size="xs" variant="subtle" color="gray"
USER                       loading={transition.isPending}
USER                       onClick={() => withdraw(r)}
USER                     >
USER                       Withdraw
USER                     </Button>
USER                   )}
USER                 </Group>
USER               ),
USER             },
USER           ]}
USER           empty={{
USER             icon: FaUserCheck,
USER             title: "You have not applied to anything yet",
USER             description: "Applications you submit will be tracked here.",
USER           }}
USER         />
USER       </Card>
USER 
USER       <HistoryDrawer
USER         applicationId={historyFor} onClose={() => setHistoryFor(null)}
USER       />
USER     </Container>
USER   );
USER }

USER modules/leave/services/applications.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Service layer for applying, substitute consent and decisions.
USER """
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER from .domain.policy import get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories, get_cl_entitlement, get_cl_lapse, get_cl_applicable_categories
USER from .domain.state_machine import Transition
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def apply_leave(principal_id: int, *, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, reason: str = None, substitute_id: int = None) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Apply for leave.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the acting person.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param reason: The reason for the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute, if applicable.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         principal = Principal(user_id=principal_id)
USER         if not get_cl_applicable_categories(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' is not applicable for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_entitlement(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Principal '{principal}' does not have entitlement for leave category '{leave_category}'.")
USER         
USER         if not get_cl_lapse(principal, leave_category):
USER             raise ValidationError(f"Leave category '{leave_category}' does not lapse for principal '{principal}'.")
USER         
USER         transition = Transition(principal, leave_category, "apply", start_date, end_date, reason)
USER         transition.apply()
USER         
USER         if substitute_id:
USER             respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent."
USER         else:
USER             unit_head_review(principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, reason)
USER             return "Leave application submitted. Awaiting Unit Head review."
USER     except ValidationError as e:
USER         return str(e)
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def respond_to_substitute_request(substitute_id: int, principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, action: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Respond to a substitute request.
USER 
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the substitute.
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param action: The action to take ('accept' or 'decline').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if action not in ["accept", "decline"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid action '{action}'. Action must be 'accept' or 'decline'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, action)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     if action == "accept":
USER         return "Substitute consent given. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER     else:
USER         return "Substitute declined. Request routed to Unit Head for final decision."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def nominate_replacement_substitute(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, substitute_id: int) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Nominate a replacement substitute.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param substitute_id: The id of the replacement substitute.
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     transition = Transition(substitute_id, principal_id, leave_category, start_date, end_date, "nominate_replacement_substitute")
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Replacement substitute '{substitute_id}' nominated for principal '{principal_id}'. Request routed to Unit Head."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Unit Head makes a final decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "unit_head_review", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Unit Head made a final decision: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def establishment_verify_and_route(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, recommendation: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Establishment verifies and routes a higher-sanction request.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param recommendation: The recommendation ('Recommended' or 'Not Recommended').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """
USER     if recommendation not in ["Recommended", "Not Recommended"]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid recommendation '{recommendation}'. Recommendation must be 'Recommended' or 'Not Recommended'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "establishment_verify_and_route", start_date, end_date, recommendation)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Establishment verified and routed the request. Recommendation: '{recommendation}' for principal '{principal_id}'."
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(principal_id: int, leave_category: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, *, decision: str) -> str:
USER     """
USER     Make a final sanctioning decision.
USER 
USER     :param principal_id: The id of the principal.
USER     :param leave_category: The leave category.
USER     :param start_date: The start date of the leave.
USER     :param end_date: The end date of the leave.
USER     :param decision: The decision ('approve' or 'reject').
USER     :return: A string indicating the result of the operation.
USER     """

USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}'. Decision must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER     
USER     transition = Transition(principal_id, leave_category, "final_sanction_decision", start_date, end_date, decision)
USER     transition.apply()
USER     
USER     return f"Final sanctioning decision made: '{decision}' for principal '{principal_id}'."

USER ops/checks/no_cross_module_fk.py
USER ```
USER #!/usr/bin/env python
USER """No ForeignKey may cross a module boundary.
USER 
USER Once an FK exists you get a join, cascade semantics and a migration
USER dependency across the boundary — and the module can never be lifted out
USER again. Cross-module references are plain integer ids instead.
USER 
USER Run:  python ops/checks/no_cross_module_fk.py
USER """
USER import os
USER import pathlib
USER import sys
USER 
USER # Runnable from anywhere: put the repo root on sys.path before importing config.
USER sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))
USER 
USER import django
USER 
USER ALLOWED_TARGETS = {"core", "contenttypes", "auth"}
USER 
USER 
USER def main() -> int:
USER     os.environ.setdefault("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", "config.settings.dev")
USER     django.setup()
USER     from django.apps import apps
USER 
USER     violations = []
USER     for model in apps.get_models():
USER         src = model._meta.app_label
USER         for f in model._meta.get_fields():
USER             if not getattr(f, "is_relation", False) or f.related_model is None:
USER                 continue
USER             if not getattr(f, "concrete", False):
USER                 continue                       # reverse accessor, not a real column
USER             dst = f.related_model._meta.app_label
USER             if dst != src and dst not in ALLOWED_TARGETS:
USER                 violations.append(f"{src}.{model.__name__}.{f.name} -> {dst}")
USER 
USER     for v in violations:
USER         print(f"cross-module FK: {v}", file=sys.stderr)
USER     if violations:
USER         print(f"\n{len(violations)} cross-module foreign key(s). "
USER               f"Use a plain id column and read via contracts.py.", file=sys.stderr)
USER         return 1
USER     print("no cross-module foreign keys")
USER     return 0
USER 
USER 
USER if __name__ == "__main__":
USER     raise SystemExit(main())
USER ```

USER ops/checks/nav_matches_routes.py
USER ```
USER #!/usr/bin/env python
USER """Every server-side nav item must have a client route, and vice versa.
USER 
USER The sidebar is built on the server (ADR-0010) and the routes live in the
USER client, with nothing linking the two at compile time. Drift is invisible in
USER review and obvious to a user: a dead link, or a page nobody can reach.
USER """
USER from __future__ import annotations
USER 
USER import importlib
USER import pathlib
USER import re
USER import sys
USER 
USER ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
USER sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
USER 
USER # Routes that exist deliberately without a nav entry.
USER EXEMPT_ROUTES = {
USER     "",           # the index redirect
USER }
USER 
USER 
USER def client_routes(module: str) -> set[str]:
USER     path = ROOT / "client" / "src" / "modules" / module / "routes.tsx"
USER     if not path.exists():
USER         return set()
USER     src = path.read_text()
USER     return {m.group(1) for m in re.finditer(r'\{\s*path:\s*"([^"]+)"', src)}
USER 
USER 
USER def main() -> int:
USER     import os
USER 
USER     import django
USER     os.environ.setdefault("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", "config.settings.dev")
USER     django.setup()
USER 
USER     from django.conf import settings
USER 
USER     problems: list[str] = []
USER     for dotted in settings.DOMAIN_MODULES:
USER         name = dotted.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
USER         try:
USER             registry = importlib.import_module(f"{dotted}.registry")
USER         except ModuleNotFoundError:
USER             continue
USER 
USER         base = registry.MODULE["base_path"].rstrip("/")
USER         nav_paths = set()
USER         for item in registry.NAV_ITEMS:
USER             to = item["to"]
USER             if not to.startswith(base):
USER                 problems.append(
USER                     f"{name}: nav item {item['code']!r} points at {to!r}, which "
USER                     f"is outside the module's base path {base!r}")
USER                 continue
USER             nav_paths.add(to[len(base):].lstrip("/"))
USER 
USER         routes = client_routes(name) | EXEMPT_ROUTES
USER 
USER         problems.extend(
USER             f"{name}: the sidebar links to {base}/{missing} but the client "
USER             f"has no route for it — the link would go nowhere"
USER             for missing in sorted(nav_paths - routes))
USER         problems.extend(
USER             f"{name}: the client has a route {base}/{orphan} with no nav "
USER             f"item — the page is unreachable from the sidebar"
USER             for orphan in sorted(routes - nav_paths - EXEMPT_ROUTES))
USER 
USER         # A nav item guarded by an unholdable permission is invisible forever.
USER         known = {code for code, _ in getattr(registry, "PERMISSIONS", [])}
USER         if known:
USER             for item in registry.NAV_ITEMS:
USER                 perm = item.get("required_permission")
USER                 if perm and perm not in known:
USER                     problems.append(
USER                         f"{name}: nav item {item['code']!r} requires {perm!r}, "
USER                         f"which is not in the module's PERMISSIONS list")
USER 
USER     if problems:
USER         print("navigation and routes disagree:\n")
USER         for p in problems:
USER             print(f"  - {p}")
USER         return 1
USER     print("nav items and client routes agree")
USER     return 0
USER 
USER 
USER if __name__ == "__main__":
USER     raise SystemExit(main())
USER ```
USER
